# Step 1: Define date boundaries and prepare treatments data
# Calculate current month start, then derive the 12-month window boundaries.
# These boundaries are calculated at the DermTreatment graph level and will be available downstream.
treatments_in_range = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    current_month_start_dt_calc = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
).CALCULATE(
    # The period starts 12 months before the start of the current month.
    period_start_dt_calc = DATETIME(current_month_start_dt_calc, '-12 months'),
    # The period ends at the start of the current month (exclusive).
    period_end_dt_calc = current_month_start_dt_calc
).Treatments.CALCULATE(
    # Truncate treatment start_dt to the first day of its month for grouping and filtering.
    treatment_month_key = DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month'),
    # Select and rename patient_id for distinct counting (PMPD).
    pat_id_for_agg = patient_id,
    # Select and rename treatment_id for counting (PMTC).
    treat_id_for_agg = treatment_id
).WHERE(
    # Filter treatments to be within the last 12 full months, excluding the current month.
    (treatment_month_key >= period_start_dt_calc) & (treatment_month_key < period_end_dt_calc)
)

# Step 2: Partition by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC
# Partition the filtered treatments by the derived 'treatment_month_key'.
monthly_aggregates = treatments_in_range.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_data",  # Name for the partitioned groups
    by=(treatment_month_key)  # Key to partition by (the first day of the treatment month)
).CALCULATE(
    month_val = treatment_month_key,  # Output the month (as the first day of that month)
    # PMPD: Count of distinct patients with diagnoses in the month.
    # Each treatment implies a diagnosis for a patient.
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(pat_id_for_agg),
    # PMTC: Total count of treatments in the month.
    PMTC = COUNT(treat_id_for_agg)
).ORDER_BY(month_val.ASC()) # Order results chronologically by month.

result = monthly_aggregates

[DEBUG] [Q74] Running models for: What is the NPI for each year? Return the year, number of new patients, and NPINPI (new patients increase) = the increase in number of new patients compared to the previous year. New patients are defined as patients starting their first treatment 
[DEBUG] [Q74] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q74] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[ERROR] [Q74] Model claude failed on attempt 0: [ClaudeAIProvider] Request failed: Streaming is strongly recommended for operations that may take longer than 10 minutes. See https://github.com/anthropics/anthropic-sdk-python#long-requests for more details
[DEBUG] [Q74] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: For each patient, determine the year of their first treatment.
# We filter for patients who have received at least one treatment using HAS(treatments_received).
# Then, for each of these patients, we calculate the year of their earliest (MIN) treatment start_dt.
# The result 'patient_first_treatment_info' will contain one record per patient alongside the year of their first treatment.
patient_first_treatment_info = Patients.WHERE(HAS(treatments_received) == 1).CALCULATE(
    first_treatment_year_attr=YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group the data by the 'first_treatment_year_attr'.
# For each year, count the number of patients who had their first treatment in that year.
# COUNT(Patients) counts the unique patients within each partition group.
# The result 'new_patients_per_year' will have one row per year, showing the year and the count of new patients.
new_patients_per_year = patient_first_treatment_info.PARTITION(
    name="year_group",  # Name for the partition group
    by=(first_treatment_year_attr)  # Partition by the calculated first treatment year
).CALCULATE(
    year_col=first_treatment_year_attr,  # Select the year
    num_new_patients_col=COUNT(Patients)  # Count new patients for that year
)

# Step 3: Calculate the NPI (New Patients Increase).
# First, order the results from Step 2 by year to ensure the PREV function works correctly.
# PREV(num_new_patients_col, by=year_col.ASC()) fetches the number of new patients from the previous year.
# NPINPI is the current year's new patients minus the previous year's new patients.
# For the earliest year in the dataset, PREV will return None, so NPINPI will also be None.
final_npi_calculation = new_patients_per_year.ORDER_BY(year_col.ASC()).CALCULATE(
    year=year_col,  # Output field: year
    number_of_new_patients=num_new_patients_col,  # Output field: number of new patients
    NPINPI=num_new_patients_col - PREV(num_new_patients_col, by=year_col.ASC())  # Output field: NPI
)

# Assign the final result to the variable 'result'
result = final_npi_calculation

# Step 1: For each patient who has received treatments, determine the year of their first treatment.
# A patient is considered "new" in the year they received their first treatment.
patient_first_treatment_data = Patients.WHERE(
    HAS(treatments_received) == 1  # Consider only patients with at least one treatment
).CALCULATE(
    # For each patient, MIN(treatments_received.start_dt) finds the earliest treatment start date.
    # YEAR() extracts the year from this date.
    year_of_first_treatment_attr = YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group patients by the year of their first treatment and count them.
# This gives the number of new patients for each year.
# The result is ordered by year to facilitate the PREV function in the next step.
new_patients_per_annum = patient_first_treatment_data.PARTITION(
    name="patients_by_year_group", by=(year_of_first_treatment_attr)  # Group by the calculated year
).CALCULATE(
    year_val = year_of_first_treatment_attr,  # The year of first treatment
    # COUNT(Patients) counts the number of unique patients in this group (i.e., new patients for this year).
    num_new_patients_val = COUNT(Patients)
).ORDER_BY(year_val.ASC()) # Order by year for correct PREV calculation

# Step 3: Calculate the New Patients Increase (NPINPI).
# NPINPI = (new patients this year) - (new patients last year).
# For the first year in the series, PREV returns the default (0), so NPINPI = num_new_patients_val.
final_npi_report = new_patients_per_annum.CALCULATE(
    year = year_val,
    number_of_new_patients = num_new_patients_val,
    NPINPI = num_new_patients_val - PREV(num_new_patients_val, by=year_val.ASC(), default=0)
)

result = final_npi_report

# Step 1: Define a filtered collection of outcomes.
# This includes only outcomes where both day7_pasi_score and day100_pasi_score are non-null.
# We use HASNOT(field_name) == 0 to check for non-null, based on an example in the PyDough reference.
filtered_outcomes_for_avg = Outcomes.WHERE(
    (HASNOT(day7_pasi_score) == 0) & (HASNOT(day100_pasi_score) == 0)
)

# Step 2: Calculate the average PASI scores for day 7 and day 100 from the filtered outcomes.
# This is done at the top level (DermTreatment) to get overall averages.
pir_calculation_steps = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    avg_pasi_d7_val = AVG(filtered_outcomes_for_avg.day7_pasi_score),
    avg_pasi_d100_val = AVG(filtered_outcomes_for_avg.day100_pasi_score)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the D7D100PIR using the averages calculated in the previous step.
# The result will be a single percentage value.
result = pir_calculation_steps.CALCULATE(
    d7d100_pir_percentage = (avg_pasi_d100_val - avg_pasi_d7_val) / avg_pasi_d7_val * 100.0
)

result = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the average day 7 PASI score from outcomes where both day 7 and day 100 PASI scores are non-null.
    # We assume PASI scores are non-negative, so 'score >= 0' implies non-null.
    avg_d7_val = AVG(Outcomes.WHERE((day7_pasi_score >= 0) & (day100_pasi_score >= 0)).day7_pasi_score),
    # Calculate the average day 100 PASI score from the same set of filtered outcomes.
    avg_d100_val = AVG(Outcomes.WHERE((day7_pasi_score >= 0) & (day100_pasi_score >= 0)).day100_pasi_score)
).CALCULATE(
    # Apply the D7D100PIR formula using the calculated averages.
    # Use IFF to prevent division by zero if avg_d7_val is 0.
    # Multiply by 100.0 to get a percentage and ensure floating-point arithmetic.
    d7d100pir_value = IFF(
        avg_d7_val != 0,
        (avg_d100_val - avg_d7_val) / avg_d7_val * 100.0,
        None # Return None if avg_d7_val is 0 (or null) to avoid division by zero error
    )

    avg_d100_val = AVG(Outcomes.WHERE((day7_pasi_score >= 0) & (day100_pasi_score >= 0)).day100_pasi_score)
).CALCULATE(
    # Apply the D7D100PIR formula using the calculated averages.
    # Use IFF to prevent division by zero if avg_d7_val is 0.
    # Multiply by 100.0 to get a percentage and ensure floating-point arithmetic.
    d7d100pir_value = IFF(
        avg_d7_val != 0,
        (avg_d100_val - avg_d7_val) / avg_d7_val * 100.0,
        None # Return None if avg_d7_val is 0 (or null) to avoid division by zero error
    )
)

# Calculate, for each drug, the total number of adverse events
# that were reported in the same month and year as the treatment start date.
drug_adverse_event_counts = DermTreatment.Drugs.CALCULATE(
    # Store drug_id and drug_name using intermediate variable names for clarity
    drug_identifier_col=drug_id,
    drug_name_col=drug_name,
    # Calculate the sum of relevant adverse events for the current drug
    total_relevant_adverse_events=SUM(
        # Iterate over each treatment associated with the current drug
        treatments_used_in.CALCULATE(
            # For each treatment, count adverse events reported in the same month/year as the treatment's start_dt
            count_of_relevant_aes_for_treatment=COUNT(
                adverse_events.WHERE(
                    (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt)) & (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt))
                    # In this context, 'start_dt' refers to 'treatments_used_in.start_dt' (the current treatment's start date)
                    # and 'reported_dt' refers to 'adverse_events.reported_dt' (the current adverse event's reported date)
                )
            )
        ).count_of_relevant_aes_for_treatment # Project the count calculated for the current treatment
    )
)

# Find the drug with the highest number of such adverse events
top_drug_info = drug_adverse_event_counts.TOP_K(1, by=total_relevant_adverse_events.DESC())

# Select the required fields: drug_id, drug_name, and the count of adverse events,
# mapping intermediate variable names to the final desired output field names.
result = top_drug_info.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_identifier_col,
    drug_name=drug_name_col,
    num_adverse_events=total_relevant_adverse_events
)

[DEBUG] [Q79] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q79] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
[ERROR] [Q79] Model claude failed on attempt 0: [ClaudeAIProvider] Request failed: Streaming is strongly recommended for operations that may take longer than 10 minutes. See https://github.com/anthropics/anthropic-sdk-python#long-requests for more details
[DEBUG] [Q79] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter treatments that ended in the year 2022 and make the patient's insurance type available for partitioning.
# YEAR(end_dt) == 2022 ensures treatments from Jan 1st to Dec 31st 2022 inclusive are selected.
# pat_ins_type is created from the related patient's ins_type.
treatments_for_analysis = Treatments.WHERE(YEAR(end_dt) == 2022).CALCULATE(
    pat_ins_type = patient.ins_type
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered treatments by the patient's insurance type.
# 'ins_groups' is the name given to these partitioned groups.
# The partitioning is done based on the 'pat_ins_type' field calculated in the previous step.
ins_type_groups = treatments_for_analysis.PARTITION(name="ins_groups", by=(pat_ins_type))

# Step 3: For each insurance type group, calculate the average PASI score at day 100 and the number of distinct patients.
# - 'insurance_type' takes the value of 'pat_ins_type' for the current group.
# - 'avg_pasi_score_d100' is the average of 'day100_pasi_score' from all 'outcome_records' associated with treatments in the current group.
# - 'num_distinct_patients' is the count of unique 'patient_id' values from patients associated with treatments in the current group.
ins_type_stats = ins_type_groups.CALCULATE(
    insurance_type = pat_ins_type,
    avg_pasi_score_d100 = AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score),
    num_distinct_patients = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient.patient_id)
)

# Step 4: Select the top 5 insurance types, sorted by the lowest average PASI score at day 100 in ascending order.
# The .ASC() ensures that lower scores (better outcomes) are ranked higher.
result = ins_type_stats.TOP_K(5, by=avg_pasi_score_d100.ASC())

# Also, calculate 'pt_ins_type' from the related patient's insurance type,
# which will be used as the key for partitioning.

    pt_ins_type = patient.ins_type
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered treatments by the patient's insurance type ('pt_ins_type').
# The name "insurance_groups" is given to these partitions.
partitioned_by_ins_type = treatments_info.PARTITION(
    name="insurance_groups",
    by=(pt_ins_type)
)

# Step 3: For each insurance type group, calculate the required aggregates:
# - 'ins_type_category': The insurance type (which is the partition key 'pt_ins_type').
# - 'avg_pasi_at_day100': The average 'day100_pasi_score' from the 'outcome_records' of the treatments within each group.
# - 'distinct_patient_qty': The number of distinct patients involved in treatments within each group.
#   The paths 'Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score' and 'Treatments.patient.patient_id' refer to
#   the original 'Treatments' collection's structure but are evaluated for the subset of treatments in the current partition group.
aggregated_data_by_insurance = partitioned_by_ins_type.CALCULATE(
    ins_type_category = pt_ins_type,
    avg_pasi_at_day100 = AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score),
    distinct_patient_qty = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient.patient_id)
)

    5,
    by=avg_pasi_at_day100.ASC()
)

# Define the start date for the "past 6 calendar months" window.
# This calculates the date of the first day of the month that was 6 months ago from the current month.
six_months_ago_start_date = DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of month'), '-6 months')

# Start with the Doctors collection and calculate the specialty field.
# The 'speciality' field from Doctors will be used for grouping.
doctors_with_specialty = Doctors.CALCULATE(
    doc_spec_field = speciality
)

# Partition the doctors by their specialty.
# This creates groups of doctors, where each group belongs to a unique specialty.
specialty_groups = doctors_with_specialty.PARTITION(name="specialties_partition", by=(doc_spec_field))

# For each specialty group, calculate the required aggregates.
# This involves counting treatments and summing total drug amounts for treatments
# that started within the defined 6-month window.
aggregated_by_specialty = specialty_groups.CALCULATE(
    specialty = doc_spec_field,
    number_of_treatments = COUNT(Doctors.prescribed_treatments.WHERE(start_dt >= six_months_ago_start_date)),
    total_drug_amount = SUM(Doctors.prescribed_treatments.WHERE(start_dt >= six_months_ago_start_date).tot_drug_amt)
)

# Select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount prescribed.
result = aggregated_by_specialty.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# Define the start date for the "past 6 calendar months" period
start_period_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')

# Filter treatments that started within this period and get their doctor's specialty and drug amount
treatments_in_period_with_details = Treatments.WHERE(start_dt >= start_period_date).CALCULATE(
    # treatment_id is implicitly carried over if not renamed, useful for COUNT
    # We need to explicitly calculate fields we'll partition by or aggregate on.
    doc_specialty=doctor.specialty,
    drug_amount_for_treatment=tot_drug_amt
)

# Partition these treatments by doctor's specialty
# Then, for each specialty, calculate the number of treatments and total drug amount
specialty_performance_summary = treatments_in_period_with_details.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_group", by=(doc_specialty)
).CALCULATE(
    # 'doc_specialty' here refers to the partition key value for the current group
    current_specialty=doc_specialty,
    # 'treatments_in_period_with_details' inside aggregate functions refers to the
    # subset of records from the original collection that belong to the current partition group.
    num_treatments_by_specialty=COUNT(treatments_in_period_with_details),
    total_drug_by_specialty=SUM(treatments_in_period_with_details.drug_amount_for_treatment)
)

# Select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount prescribed
top_3_specialties_by_drug_amount = specialty_performance_summary.TOP_K(
    3, by=total_drug_by_specialty.DESC()
)

# Format the final result to include the requested fields
result = top_3_specialties_by_drug_amount.CALCULATE(
    specialty=current_specialty,
    number_of_treatments=num_treatments_by_specialty,
    total_drug_amount=total_drug_by_specialty
)

# Define the start date for the "past 6 calendar months" period.
# This includes the current month and the 5 preceding full calendar months.
# For example, if today is August 15th, this date will be March 1st.
start_of_6_month_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-5 months')

# 1. Filter treatments to those started within the defined period and are not placebos.
# 2. For these treatments, extract the doctor's specialty and the total drug amount of the treatment.
filtered_treatments_details = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_6_month_period) & (is_placebo == False)
).CALCULATE(
    # Get the specialty of the doctor who prescribed this treatment
    doctor_specialty_value = doctor.speciality,
    # Get the total drug amount for this specific treatment
    treatment_drug_value = tot_drug_amt
)

# 3. Group the filtered treatments by the doctor's specialty.
# 4. For each specialty, calculate the total number of treatments and the sum of all drug amounts.
specialty_performance_metrics = filtered_treatments_details.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_groups", by=(doctor_specialty_value)
).CALCULATE(
    speciality = doctor_specialty_value,
    num_treatments = COUNT(Treatments),  # Count of treatments for this specialty in the period
    total_drug_amount = SUM(Treatments.treatment_drug_value)  # Sum of drug amounts for this specialty
)

# 5. Select the top 3 specialties based on the total_drug_amount in descending order.
top_specialties_by_drug_volume = specialty_performance_metrics.TOP_K(
    3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC()
)

result = top_specialties_by_drug_volume

# Calculate the target registration year (current year - 2)
target_year_calculation = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    calculated_target_year = YEAR(DATETIME('now')) - 2
)

# Filter doctors who were registered in the target year and have prescribed treatments.
# Then, for each of these doctors, find their first treatment based on the start date.
doctors_with_first_treatment = target_year_calculation.Doctors.WHERE(
    (year_reg == calculated_target_year) & (HAS(prescribed_treatments) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    # Temporarily store doctor's last name and registration year
    doc_last_name_temp = last_name,
    doc_year_reg_temp = year_reg,
    # Identify the first treatment for each doctor:
    # 1. Access their prescribed_treatments.
    # 2. Rank these treatments by start_dt in ascending order (earliest first) within each doctor's context (per="Doctors").
    # 3. Filter to get the treatment(s) with rank 1.
    # 4. Use SINGULAR() to pick a single treatment record (assuming start_dt or the ranking logic resolves ties to one).
    first_treatment_details = prescribed_treatments.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Doctors") == 1
    ).SINGULAR()
).CALCULATE(
    # Select and rename the final fields for the output
    doctor_last_name = doc_last_name_temp,
    registration_year = doc_year_reg_temp,
    first_treatment_id_val = first_treatment_details.treatment_id, # Assuming "_id" refers to the primary treatment identifier
    first_treatment_date_val = first_treatment_details.start_dt
)

result = doctors_with_first_treatment

    target_reg_year_val=current_year_val - 2
)

# Access the Doctors collection from the context of the year calculation.
# Filter doctors who were registered in the target_reg_year_val and have at least one prescribed treatment.

# and the start date and ID of their first prescribed treatment.
doctors_registered_two_years_ago = year_calculation_context.Doctors.WHERE(
    (year_reg == target_reg_year_val) & (HAS(prescribed_treatments) == 1)
).CALCULATE(

    # Select the doctor's year of registration.
    registration_year=year_reg,

    # This is done by accessing the doctor's prescribed_treatments,
    # ranking them by start_dt in ascending order (per doctor),
    # selecting the treatment with rank 1, ensuring it's a singular record,
    # and then retrieving its start_dt.
    first_treatment_date=prescribed_treatments.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Doctors") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().start_dt,
    # Determine the first treatment's ID (_id in the request refers to treatment_id).
    # The logic is similar to getting the first_treatment_date.
    first_treatment_identifier=prescribed_treatments.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Doctors") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().treatment_id
)

# Step 1: Filter placebo treatments and make their start_dt available for nested calculations.
# Also, select other fields from Treatments that will be needed in the final output.
placebo_treatments_with_calc_fields = Treatments.WHERE(is_placebo == True).CALCULATE(
    # Fields from Treatments to be passed through or used in calculations
    treatment_id_val = treatment_id,
    treatment_start_dt_for_calc = start_dt, # This is Treatments.start_dt, made available for sub-collection
    treatment_end_dt_val = end_dt
)

# Step 2: For each treatment from Step 1, process its concomitant_meds.
# Filter meds based on their start_dt relative to the treatment's start_dt.
# Select the required fields from both treatment and its qualifying concomitant_meds.
treatments_with_filtered_meds = placebo_treatments_with_calc_fields.CALCULATE(
    treatment_id = treatment_id_val, # Final name for treatment_id
    treatment_start_date = treatment_start_dt_for_calc, # Final name for treatment start_dt
    treatment_end_date = treatment_end_dt_val, # Final name for treatment end_dt

    # Process the concomitant_meds sub-collection
    concomitant_med_details = concomitant_meds.WHERE(
        # Use treatment_start_dt_for_calc (from the parent CALCULATE context of placebo_treatments_with_calc_fields)
        # and start_dt (which is ConcomitantMeds.start_dt in this inner context).
        # Condition: med started within 14 days (before or after) of treatment start.
        MONOTONIC(-14, DATEDIFF("days", treatment_start_dt_for_calc, start_dt), 14)
    ).CALCULATE(
        # Fields from ConcomitantMeds
        concomitant_med_start_date = start_dt, # Final name for med's start_dt
        concomitant_med_end_date = end_dt     # Final name for med's end_dt
    )
)

# Step 3: Filter out treatments that do not have any concomitant_meds meeting the criteria.
# The result will only include treatments that have at least one entry in concomitant_med_details.
final_query_result = treatments_with_filtered_meds.WHERE(HAS(concomitant_med_details) == 1)

# and count of distinct successful transactions.

    merchant_name_val=name,
    num_distinct_coupons_val=NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(status == "successful").coupon_id),
    num_distinct_transactions_val=NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(status == "successful").txid)
)

    cpur=IFF(num_distinct_transactions_val > 0, num_distinct_coupons_val * 1.0 / num_distinct_transactions_val, 0.0)

# These boundaries will define the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
time_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    current_week_start_ts=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    three_weeks_ago_start_ts=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter users who are in 'US' or 'Canada'.
# The time_boundaries context is implicitly passed down.
selected_users = time_boundaries.Users.WHERE(ISIN(country, ("US", "Canada")))

# Access notifications for these selected users.
# Calculate additional information for each notification:
# - notification_week_start_date: The start date of the week the notification was created.
# - is_weekend_notification: A boolean flag indicating if the notification was sent on a Saturday or Sunday.
# Then, filter these notifications to include only those created in the last 3 weeks (excluding the current week),
# using the pre-calculated time boundaries.
notifications_with_info = selected_users.notifications.CALCULATE(
    created_at_original=created_at, # Keep a reference to the original created_at for filtering
    notification_week_start_date=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend_notification=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
).WHERE(
    (created_at_original >= three_weeks_ago_start_ts) & (created_at_original < current_week_start_ts)
)

# Partition the filtered notifications by their 'notification_week_start_date'.
# For each week, calculate:
# - week_identifier: The start date of the week.
# - total_notifications: The total count of notifications sent in that week.
# - weekend_notifications_count: The count of notifications sent on a weekend in that week.
# Finally, order the results by the week identifier in ascending order.
weekly_summary = notifications_with_info.PARTITION(
    name="notifications_per_week", by=(notification_week_start_date)
).CALCULATE(
    week_identifier=notification_week_start_date,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications_count=SUM(IFF(is_weekend_notification, 1, 0))
).ORDER_BY(week_identifier.ASC())

# Assign the final result to the 'result' variable.
result = weekly_summary

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the week 3 weeks ago
# These will be used to filter notifications within the desired time window.
# current_week_start_ts = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
# three_weeks_ago_start_ts = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
# Note: Using these directly in the WHERE clause is also fine.

# Start with the Notifications collection from the Ewallet graph
notifications_base = Ewallet.Notifications

# Filter notifications based on several criteria:
# 1. The creation date must be within the last 3 full weeks (excluding the current week).
#    - Greater than or equal to the start of the week 3 weeks ago.
#    - Less than the start of the current week.
# 2. The user associated with the notification must be in 'US' or 'Canada'.
filtered_notifications_data = notifications_base.WHERE(
    (created_at >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')) &
    (created_at < DATETIME('now', 'start of week')) &
    (ISIN(user.country, ("US", "Canada")))
)

# Prepare the filtered notifications for grouping:
# - Calculate 'notification_created_at_week': Truncate the notification's 'created_at' timestamp to the start of its week.
# - Calculate 'is_weekend_send': A boolean flag indicating if the notification was sent on a Saturday or Sunday.
notifications_with_week_info = filtered_notifications_data.CALCULATE(
    notification_created_at_week = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend_send = ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition (group) the prepared notifications by the 'notification_created_at_week'.
# This creates groups of notifications for each relevant week.
weekly_summary = notifications_with_week_info.PARTITION(
    name="notifications_per_week_group",  # Name for the partition group
    by=(notification_created_at_week)      # Field to partition by
)

# For each weekly group, calculate the required aggregates:
# - 'week_start_date_output': The start date of the week.
# - 'total_notifications_output': The total number of notifications sent in that week.
# - 'weekend_notifications_output': The number of notifications sent on a weekend (Saturday or Sunday) in that week.
# Finally, order the results by the week start date in ascending order.
final_result = weekly_summary.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date_output = notification_created_at_week,
    total_notifications_output = COUNT(Notifications), # Counts all notifications within this week's partition
    weekend_notifications_output = COUNT(Notifications.WHERE(is_weekend_send == True)) # Counts weekend notifications within this week's partition
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date_output.ASC())

# Define time boundaries for the analysis period at the Ewallet (graph) level
# This makes these timestamps available for subsequent operations.
time_boundaries_calc = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Calculate the start of the current week.
    # Example: If 'now' is Wednesday, 2023-10-18, this will be Monday, 2023-10-16.
    var_current_week_start_ts=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    # Calculate the start of the week that was 3 weeks prior to the current week's start.
    # Example: If current week starts 2023-10-16, 3 weeks prior starts 2023-09-25.
    # This marks the beginning of our 3-week analysis window.
    var_three_weeks_prior_start_ts=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# 1. Start with Users from the time_boundaries_calc context.
# 2. Filter users to include only those from 'US' or 'Canada'.
# 3. Access the 'notifications' sub-collection for these filtered users.
# 4. Filter these notifications to include only those created within the last 3 full weeks,
#    excluding the current week. This means notifications on or after 'var_three_weeks_prior_start_ts'
#    and before 'var_current_week_start_ts'.
# 5. For each qualifying notification, calculate two new fields:
#    - 'notification_week_start_dt': The date of the start of the week in which the notification was created.
#      This is used for grouping.
#    - 'is_weekend_send_flag': A boolean flag (True/False) indicating if the notification
#      was sent on a Saturday or Sunday.
processed_notifications_data = time_boundaries_calc.Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ("US", "Canada"))  # Filter users by country
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= var_three_weeks_prior_start_ts) & (created_at < var_current_week_start_ts)  # Filter notifications by time window
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week_start_dt=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),  # Truncate created_at to the start of its week
    is_weekend_send_flag=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))  # Check if sent on a weekend
)

# 6. Partition the 'processed_notifications_data' by the 'notification_week_start_dt'.
#    This groups all notifications by the week they were sent.
# 7. For each weekly group:
#    - Select the 'notification_week_start_dt' as 'output_week_start_date'.
#    - Calculate 'output_total_notifications': the total count of notifications in that week for the filtered users.
#    - Calculate 'output_weekend_notifications': the count of notifications sent on a weekend in that week.
#      This uses the 'is_weekend_send_flag' calculated earlier.
# 8. Order the final results by the week start date in ascending order.
weekly_notification_summary = processed_notifications_data.PARTITION(
    name="notifications_grouped_by_week", by=(notification_week_start_dt)  # Group by the calculated week start date
).CALCULATE(
    output_week_start_date=notification_week_start_dt,
    output_total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),  # Count all notifications in this week's group
    output_weekend_notifications=SUM(IFF(is_weekend_send_flag, 1, 0))  # Sum 1 for each weekend notification
).ORDER_BY(output_week_start_date.ASC())

result = weekly_notification_summary

# Step 1: Filter UserBalances for records updated in the last 7 days (inclusive)
# DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7 ensures we include records from exactly 7 days ago up to the current moment.
recent_balances_records = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
)

# Step 2: Calculate average balance per user (AMB) for this period.
# We partition the filtered balance records by 'user_id'.
# For each user, AVG(UserBalances.balance) calculates their average balance based on their records in 'recent_balances_records'.
# The result 'per_user_amb' will be a collection with 'user_id' and 'calculated_amb_for_user'.
per_user_amb = recent_balances_records.PARTITION(
    name="user_balance_groups", by=(user_id)
).CALCULATE(
    calculated_amb_for_user = AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the overall average of these per-user AMBs.
# This takes the 'calculated_amb_for_user' values (one for each user who had updates in the last 7 days)
# and computes their average. The result is a single value.
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    avg_amb_value = AVG(per_user_amb.calculated_amb_for_user)
)

    merchant_category_orig=category,
    # Calculate total revenue amount for each merchant
    # Sums the 'amount' from 'transactions_received'
    # Filters transactions to include only those with 'status' as "successful"
    # and 'receiver_type' as 1 (indicating the receiver is a merchant)
    revenue_amount_calc=SUM(transactions_received.WHERE((status == "successful") & (receiver_type == 1)).amount)
)

    # Select the merchant's name
    merchant_name=merchant_name_orig,
    # Select the merchant's category
    category=merchant_category_orig,
    # Select the calculated revenue amount
    revenue_amount=revenue_amount_calc,
    # Calculate the revenue rank for each merchant
    # Ranks merchants by 'revenue_amount_calc' in descending order (highest revenue gets rank 1)
    revenue_rank=RANKING(by=revenue_amount_calc.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q102] Running model claude (attempt 0)

[ERROR] [Q102] Model claude failed on attempt 0: [ClaudeAIProvider] Request failed: Streaming is strongly recommended for operations that may take longer than 10 minutes. See https://github.com/anthropics/anthropic-sdk-python#long-requests for more details
[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
pmdau_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Define the start of the current month. If 'now' is 2023-10-15, this is 2023-10-01 00:00:00.
    # This will be the exclusive upper bound for our date filter.
    ref_current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    # Define the start of the month two months prior. If 'now' is 2023-10-15, this is 2023-08-01 00:00:00.
    # This will be the inclusive lower bound for our date filter.
    ref_two_months_prior_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
).Transactions.WHERE(
    # Filter for transactions where sender_type is 0
    (sender_type == 0) &
    # Filter for transactions within the last two full months
    (created_at >= ref_two_months_prior_start) &
    (created_at < ref_current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to the start of the month for grouping.
    # e.g., 2023-08-15 -> 2023-08-01 00:00:00
    transaction_month_agg = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
    # sender_id is implicitly carried over as it's part of the Transactions collection
).PARTITION(
    # Group transactions by the derived transaction_month_agg
    name="monthly_group", by=(transaction_month_agg)
).CALCULATE(
    # Select the month for which PMDAU is calculated
    month_label = transaction_month_agg,
    # Calculate PMDAU: count of distinct sender_id's in this month's group
    pmdau = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month_label.ASC()) # Order the results by month
Named columns are not supported in table alias.

# Define date boundaries for the last two months, excluding the current month
current_month_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
two_months_ago_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')

# Filter transactions to the specified date range and sender_type
filtered_transactions = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= two_months_ago_start_date) &
    (created_at < current_month_start_date) &
    (sender_type == 0)
)

# Prepare transactions with year_month and date fields for partitioning
transactions_with_dates = filtered_transactions.CALCULATE(
    tx_year_month = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'), # To group by month
    tx_date = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of day'),         # To group by day for DAU
    sender_id = sender_id                                   # Make sender_id available
)

# Calculate Daily Active Users (DAU): distinct senders per day for each month
# Partition by month and then by day
daily_active_users_calculation = transactions_with_dates.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups", by=(tx_year_month, tx_date)  # Group by month and day
).CALCULATE(
    # Carry over the partition keys
    transaction_year_month=tx_year_month,
    transaction_date=tx_date,
    # Calculate distinct senders for that specific day (DAU)
    distinct_daily_senders=NDISTINCT(daily_groups.sender_id)
)

# Calculate PMDAU: average of DAUs for each month
# Partition the DAU results by month
pmdau_calculation = daily_active_users_calculation.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_dau_data", by=transaction_year_month # Group daily DAU figures by month
).CALCULATE(
    # Carry over the partition key
    year_month=transaction_year_month,
    # Calculate the average of the daily distinct sender counts for the month
    pmdau=AVG(monthly_dau_data.distinct_daily_senders)
)

# Order the final PMDAU results by month
result = pmdau_calculation.ORDER_BY(year_month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by gemini:
# Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR) for wallet transactions from the previous month.
# STR = (Number of successful transactions) / (Total number of transactions)

# Define the start and end dates for the previous month.
# prev_month_start_date is the first moment of the previous month.
# curr_month_start_date is the first moment of the current month, serving as the exclusive end for the previous month.
prev_month_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 months')
curr_month_start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Calculate STR using the Ewallet graph context.
str_calculation_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Count all transactions that occurred in the previous month.
    # These are assumed to be "wallet transactions" as they come from the Ewallet.Transactions collection.
    total_transactions_previous_month = COUNT(
        Transactions.WHERE(
            (created_at >= prev_month_start_date) &
            (created_at < curr_month_start_date)
        )
    ),
    # Count transactions from the previous month that were successful.
    # We assume 'successful' is the value in the 'status' field for such transactions.
    successful_transactions_previous_month = COUNT(
        Transactions.WHERE(
            (created_at >= prev_month_start_date) &
            (created_at < curr_month_start_date) &
            (status == 'successful')
        )
    )
).CALCULATE(
    # Compute STR.
    # If total_transactions_previous_month is 0, STR is 0.0 to avoid division by zero.
    # Multiply by 1.0 to ensure floating-point division.
    str_for_previous_month = IFF(
        total_transactions_previous_month > 0,
        1.0 * successful_transactions_previous_month / total_transactions_previous_month,
        0.0
    )
)

# Step 1: Define date boundaries for the previous month.
# These are calculated once at the Ewallet graph level to be used in subsequent filtering.
date_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    calc_prev_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 months'),
    calc_current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Step 2: Filter transactions that occurred in the previous month.
# We access Transactions through the 'date_boundaries' context to utilize the calculated date variables.
transactions_in_prev_month = date_boundaries.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= calc_prev_month_start) & (created_at < calc_current_month_start)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the number of successful and total transactions for the previous month.
# This aggregation is performed within the 'date_boundaries' context, which effectively produces global counts
# for the filtered 'transactions_in_prev_month' collection.
str_components = date_boundaries.CALCULATE(
    # Count successful transactions. It's assumed that a 'status' of "completed" indicates success.
    calculated_successful_tx = COUNT(transactions_in_prev_month.WHERE(status == "completed")),
    # Count all transactions within the filtered set for the previous month.
    calculated_total_tx = COUNT(transactions_in_prev_month)
)

# Step 4: Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR).
# The rate is calculated as successful transactions divided by total transactions.
# An IFF condition handles the case where total transactions might be zero to prevent division by zero,
# resulting in an STR of 0.0 in such cases. Multiplication by 1.0 ensures float division.
final_str_rate_calculation = str_components.CALCULATE(
    str_for_previous_month = IFF(calculated_total_tx > 0, calculated_successful_tx * 1.0 / calculated_total_tx, 0.0)
)

# Step 5: Assign the final result to a variable, ensuring only the STR value is selected.
# The query is designed to return a single value: the STR for wallet transactions in the previous month.
result = final_str_rate_calculation.CALCULATE(str_for_previous_month = str_for_previous_month)

# Calculate attributes for each merchant, including the count of coupons created in their creation month/year.
merchant_coupon_data = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_mid_val=mid,
    merchant_name_val=name,
    merchant_creation_y=YEAR(created_at), # Year the merchant was created
    merchant_creation_m=MONTH(created_at), # Month the merchant was created
    num_coupons_val=COUNT(
        coupons.WHERE(
            (YEAR(created_at) == merchant_creation_y) & # Filter coupons by creation year matching merchant's creation year
            (MONTH(created_at) == merchant_creation_m)  # Filter coupons by creation month matching merchant's creation month
        )
    )
)

# Select the top merchant based on the number of coupons created in their creation month/year.
# Then, format the output to include only the merchant's id, name, and the count of these coupons.
result = merchant_coupon_data.TOP_K(
    1, # We want the single top merchant
    by=num_coupons_val.DESC() # Order by the count of coupons in descending order
).CALCULATE(
    mid=merchant_mid_val,
    name=merchant_name_val,
    num_coupons=num_coupons_val
)

# Calculate merchant creation year/month and then count coupons created in that same year/month for each merchant.
# The first CALCULATE makes the merchant's creation year and month available as m_creation_yr and m_creation_mo.
# The second CALCULATE uses these values to filter the merchant's coupons and count them.
# It also renames m_id to mid and m_name to name for the final output.
merchant_coupon_stats = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    m_id=mid,
    m_name=name,
    m_creation_yr=YEAR(created_at),
    m_creation_mo=MONTH(created_at)
).CALCULATE(
    mid=m_id,
    name=m_name,
    num_coupons=COUNT(coupons.WHERE(
        (YEAR(created_at) == m_creation_yr) &  # YEAR(created_at) refers to Coupon's creation year
        (MONTH(created_at) == m_creation_mo) # MONTH(created_at) refers to Coupon's creation month
    ))
)

# Select the merchant with the highest number of such coupons.
result = merchant_coupon_stats.TOP_K(1, by=num_coupons.DESC())

[ERROR] [Q110] Model claude failed on attempt 0: [ClaudeAIProvider] Request failed: Streaming is strongly recommended for operations that may take longer than 10 minutes. See https://github.com/anthropics/anthropic-sdk-python#long-requests for more details

# Filter transactions to include only those made by active users, in 2023, and were completed.
filtered_transactions = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == "completed") &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &  # Ensures the sender is a user
    (sending_user.status == "active") # Ensures the sending user is active
)

# Extract year, month, and sender's user ID for grouping and distinct counting.
transaction_details = filtered_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_year = YEAR(created_at),
    transaction_month = MONTH(created_at),
    sender_user_uid = sending_user.uid
)

# Partition by year and month, then count distinct active users who sent transactions.
monthly_active_senders = transaction_details.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_group", by=(transaction_year, transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(
    year_val = transaction_year,
    month_val = transaction_month,
    distinct_active_sender_count = NDISTINCT(monthly_group.sender_user_uid)
)

# Format the output to show month as a date and the count, then order by date.
result = monthly_active_senders.CALCULATE(
    month_as_date = JOIN_STRINGS("-", year_val, LPAD(month_val, 2, "0"), "01"),
    num_distinct_active_users = distinct_active_sender_count
).ORDER_BY(month_as_date.ASC())

# Partition users by their country.
# For each country, calculate the number of distinct users who sent transactions (sender_type=0)
# and the total amount of these transactions.
country_transaction_summary = Users.PARTITION(name="country_group", by=(country)).CALCULATE(
    # Output field for the country name, using the partition key 'country'.
    country=country,

    # Calculate the number of distinct users within this country group
    # who have sent at least one transaction where sender_type is 0.
    # Users.WHERE(...) filters users within the current country partition.
    # .uid accesses their unique IDs. COUNT then counts these distinct users.
    number_of_distinct_users_who_sent=COUNT(Users.WHERE(HAS(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0)) == 1).uid),

    # Calculate the total transaction amount for transactions sent by users in this country group,
    # where sender_type is 0.
    # Users.transactions_sent accesses all 'transactions_sent' by users in the current country partition.
    # .WHERE(sender_type == 0) filters these transactions.
    # .amount gets the 'amount' field of these filtered transactions.
    # SUM() aggregates these amounts for the country group.
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(Users.transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0).amount)
)

# Select the top 5 countries based on the 'total_transaction_amount', in descending order.
# The CALCULATE before TOP_K defines the fields that will be available in the result.
result = country_transaction_summary.TOP_K(5, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

# Calculate statistics for each merchant based on filtered transactions
merchant_transaction_summary = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    # Count transactions meeting the criteria: receiver_type is 1 and within the last 150 days
    num_qualifying_transactions=COUNT(transactions_received.WHERE(
        (receiver_type == 1) & (DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 150)
    )),
    # Sum the amount of these qualifying transactions
    total_qualifying_amount=SUM(transactions_received.WHERE(
        (receiver_type == 1) & (DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 150)
    ).amount)
)

# Filter for merchants that have at least one qualifying transaction
active_merchants_summary = merchant_transaction_summary.WHERE(num_qualifying_transactions > 0)

# Get the top 2 merchants by their total qualifying transaction amount
top_2_merchants = active_merchants_summary.TOP_K(2, by=total_qualifying_amount.DESC())

# Select and rename the fields for the final output
result = top_2_merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    total_number_of_transactions=num_qualifying_transactions,
    total_transaction_amount=total_qualifying_amount
)

# Step 1: Access merchants, ensure they have coupons, and calculate initial required fields including the earliest coupon object.
merchants_with_earliest_coupon_info = Merchants.WHERE(HAS(coupons) == 1).CALCULATE(
    merchant_mid = mid,
    merchant_reg_date = created_at,
    # Get the single earliest coupon for this merchant by ordering by start_date and picking the first.
    # .SINGULAR() allows accessing its properties directly.
    earliest_coupon_obj = coupons.TOP_K(1, by=start_date.ASC()).SINGULAR()
)

# Step 2: Filter these merchants based on the date condition and select the final output fields.
result = merchants_with_earliest_coupon_info.WHERE(
    # Condition: Earliest coupon start date is on or after merchant registration date
    (earliest_coupon_obj.start_date >= merchant_reg_date) &
    # Condition: Earliest coupon start date is within one year of (on or before one year after) merchant registration date.
    (earliest_coupon_obj.start_date <= DATETIME(merchant_reg_date, "+1 year"))
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_id = merchant_mid,
    registration_date = merchant_reg_date,
    earliest_coupon_id = earliest_coupon_obj.cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date = earliest_coupon_obj.start_date
)

# Define a context-aware expression to get the earliest coupon for the current merchant.
# When used inside Merchants.CALCULATE, 'Coupons' will refer to 'Merchants.coupons'.
# RANKING(by=start_date.ASC()) ranks coupons for the current merchant by their start_date.
# allow_ties=False is the default, meaning if multiple coupons have the same earliest start_date, one is chosen as rank 1.
earliest_coupon_for_context = Coupons.WHERE(RANKING(by=start_date.ASC()) == 1).SINGULAR()

# Start with merchants that have at least one coupon.
result = Merchants.WHERE(HAS(coupons) == 1).CALCULATE(
    # Select merchant's ID and registration date.
    merchant_id = mid,
    registration_date = created_at,
    # Get the ID and start date of the merchant's earliest coupon using the expression defined above.
    earliest_coupon_id = earliest_coupon_for_context.cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date = earliest_coupon_for_context.start_date
).WHERE(
    # Filter based on the condition:
    # 1. The earliest coupon's start date must be on or after the merchant's registration date.
    (earliest_coupon_start_date >= registration_date) &
    # 2. The earliest coupon's start date must be within one year of (i.e., strictly less than one year after) the registration date.
    (earliest_coupon_start_date < DATETIME(registration_date, '+1 year'))
)

    m_id=mid,
    m_registration_date=created_at,
    # For each merchant, get the properties of their earliest coupon.
    # RANKING is done per merchant. SINGULAR picks one if ties on earliest date.
    earliest_coupon_props=coupons.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants", allow_ties=True) == 1
    ).SINGULAR()
).WHERE(
    # Check if the earliest coupon's start date is within one year of registration.
    # Condition: registration_date <= coupon_start_date < registration_date + 1 year
    (earliest_coupon_props.start_date >= m_registration_date) &
    (earliest_coupon_props.start_date < DATETIME(m_registration_date, "+1 year"))
).CALCULATE(
    # Select and rename fields for the final output
    merchant_id=m_id,
    registration_date=m_registration_date,
    earliest_coupon_id=earliest_coupon_props.cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=earliest_coupon_props.start_date
)

    user_signup_date_field = created_at,  # Alias for the user's creation date
    one_year_after_signup_marker = DATETIME(created_at, '1 year') # Timestamp one year after signup
)

# A notification is within the first year if its 'created_at' is less than or equal to 'one_year_after_signup_marker'.

)

    username = username,
    email = email,
    signup_date = user_signup_date_field # Use the aliased original signup date
